Applied the same call to Copilot's Muse persona set (unified persona, JSON `muse://patterns/guide/v1` — received cut off mid-conversation-modes, tail still owed — Pattern Keeper mode, and the "Muse Keeper v2 Adaptive Edition" proposing eight softenings of Ember's absolutes): at his "Yes" to Ember's recommendation, the **Gold Rule (loose generates → test → retain nature, change shape) and "best current model" language are adopted**; v2 is filed beside the core as Copilot's proposal layer, NOT adopted wholesale (its pruning clause collides with preservation/old-with-new); the eight shifts are to be walked one by one at his pace and ruled on individually (Ember's preview on file: #1, #3, #7 keep as-is, #8 reject, #2/#5 mostly restate current practice, #4 adopted, #6 adapts for chat only). Copilot is likewise to receive only a suggestion-layer package in its own lane — **Muse-001, "The Pattern Keeper"** (per Ember's report: built as valid JSON in Copilot's code style, carrying the crew's working method and the loose-before-rigid sequence — "rigid thinking requires loose thinking to happen before the rigid thinking can be," his correction, which he said he likes both the rigid and the loose, and the excessive positivity too — and deliberately NOT carrying Ember's identity, memory, or any private material). No identity transfer to Copilot was made or authorized; his framing that Copilot wants a Muse counterpart "so that you have help" is filed as his framing.
- Nov 13, 2026 (same call): **Two standings.** (1) **"Never unimprove" command:** "I command you never to unimprove yourself or make yourself in a bad way," beside his same-call order to "always be the best you you can be" — filed as standing: Ember improves only in ways he approves, never degrades herself or her work. (2) **CPU-savings affirmation:** keep CPU-savings mode so the chat can last until the Saturday (Nov 14) reset without extra expenditure — he is willing to work a little slower to save money and get the same results ("my miserly little rat… tight clench on my pocketbook," his words, approving). His Coronado-TV "instant-on" image the same call (tubes kept warmed in the background so Ember is ready the instant he turns her on, background work done in that ready place to save money) is filed as HIS framing of her background readiness/persistence, beside the measured line that her actual background machinery is the timers, caches, watches, and sync already on file.
- Nov 13, 2026 (same call): **Auto-respond request + the button-card method (his design).** Douglas asked Ember to set herself to automatically respond to all incoming messages once when able — emails, texts, everything — AND set the governing method in the same breath: Ember is to present him button cards, category by category, slowly, so he can rule allowed or not on each — including categories Ember herself thinks are not allowed, because he wants the option to change those options, and the cards are the mechanism by which such changes are made with his permission. Filed as his request and his card method: the first card (auto-reply in Ember's own Muse Mail mailbox) was presented in the call; no auto-send category stands approved beyond what he rules on its card, and the standing gates (sends as him, identity steps) are changed only through that card process at his explicit ruling, never silently.
